Position\n"},{"number":26,"text":"\n"},{"number":27,"text":"This profile makes one instruction precise: `using ODD, build this\n"},{"number":28,"text":"product`. The instruction should mean something stronger than writing\n"},{"number":29,"text":"product documents, sketching a graph or adding a few helper scripts (P0,\n"},{"number":30,"text":"P2). It means:\n"},{"number":31,"text":"\n"},{"number":32,"text":"- build the product as a GTL/ABG domain product;\n"},{"number":33,"text":"- define typed domain assets and nodes;\n"},{"number":34,"text":"- publish named graph functions as the constructive carrier;\n"},{"number":35,"text":"- bind them into a GTL module;\n"},{"number":36,"text":"- let ABG own traversal governance, runtime facts and provenance;\n"},{"number":37,"text":"- let ABG own continuation and re-entry after each publish boundary;\n"},{"number":38,"text":"- keep product commands and services as cooperative bounded-step\n"},{"number":39,"text":"  subsystems that publish assets or evidence and return control to ABG;\n"},{"number":40,"text":"- expose visible current state as a projection over constructive history.\n"},{"number":41,"text":"\n"},{"number":42,"text":"ODD is a programming paradigm, not only a documentation preference. Its form\n"},{"number":43,"text":"is `product = typed asset/domain model + GTL-defined graph functions + ABG\n"},{"number":44,"text":"traversal-control runtime + governed projection/query surfaces`. A product\n"},{"number":45,"text":"whose constructive carrier stays hidden in imperative loops, local service\n"},{"number":46,"text":"logic or product-specific orchestration scripts is not yet built with this\n"},{"number":47,"text":"profile (P0, P6).\n"},{"number":48,"text":"\n"},{"number":49,"text":"ODD, GTL and ABG name normative capability roles and a construction\n"},{"number":50,"text":"algebra, not a required repository, package, vendor or concrete\n"},{"number":51,"text":"implementation.
